# GitHub Issue Resolution Progress Tracker

> Master Mission: Systematically resolve genuine GitHub Issues with production-quality solutions, comprehensive automated tests, dedicated feature branches, and verified Pull Requests submitted to upstream `code-with-abhi-i5/CodePulse:main`.

- **Target Issues**: Up to 500 Issues

- **Active Assigned Pipeline**: 692 Issues in `code-with-dipak-777`
- **Current Active Batch**: Batch 1 (Issues #6617 - #6599: Enterprise SRE & Infrastructure Systems)
